import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
    del up_output
    activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )
        del packed_output

codex
v141 is a packed dense BMM design: pack into `[E,192,H]`, two FC1 BMM allocations, in-place SwiGLU into `gate_output`, then chunked FC2 BMM allocations and weighted unpack. I’m checking whether preallocation or `out=` variants were already tried, because that is the most plausible allocator-pressure architectural change that still preserves the exact padded tensors.

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    workspace = _WORKSPACE_CACHE.get(key)
    if workspace is None:
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        if num_experts == 32:
            gate_output = torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            up_output = torch.empty_like(gate_output)
        else:
            gate_output = None
            up_output = None
        workspace = (packed_input, gate_output, up_output)
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE[key] = workspace
    return workspace


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, gate_workspace, up_workspace = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    if num_experts == 32:
        torch.bmm(packed_input, gate_w.transpose(1, 2), out=gate_workspace)
        torch.bmm(packed_input, up_w.transpose(1, 2), out=up_workspace)
        swiglu(gate_workspace, up_workspace, gate_workspace)
        activation = gate_workspace
    else:
        gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
        up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
        swiglu(gate_output, up_output, gate_output)
        activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, gate_output)
    activation = gate_output
    down_w_t = down_w.transpose(1, 2)
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w_t[expert_start:expert_end]
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = num_experts == 16
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    workspace = _WORKSPACE_CACHE.get(key)
    if workspace is None:
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if num_experts == 16
            else None
        )
        workspace = (packed_input, activation)
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE[key] = workspace
    return workspace


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    pack, swiglu, combine_weights, combined_swiglu, unpack_chunks = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        expert_block_m,
    )

    pack(
        stacked_expert_tokens,
        group_sizes,
        group_padded_offsets,
        packed_input,
    )
    if num_experts == 16:
        combined_w = _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights)
        gate_up_output = packed_input @ combined_w.transpose(1, 2)
        combined_swiglu(gate_up_output, gate_up_output[:, :, :intermediate])
        activation = gate_up_output[:, :, :intermediate]
    else:
        gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
        up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
        swiglu(gate_output, up_output, gate_output)
        activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE = {}
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )
                else:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = T.float16(0)

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = num_experts == 64
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels

CANDIDATE_RANKING

1. `v149_swiglu_group_sizes_metadata`: change `_swiglu_kernel` to consume `group_sizes` directly instead of deriving `group_size = group_offsets[e+1] - group_offsets[e]`.
Evidence: v136 proved guarded SwiGLU is correct and slightly beneficial; v141 keeps that path. This is a fresh metadata/dataflow simplification, preserves M192 and all BMM shapes, adds no launches, allocates no new tensor, and avoids two int metadata loads plus one subtract per expert/block.

2. Scoped per-call FC2 transpose materialization for one case only.
Rejected for v149. v134 local transpose view was slower, and contiguous materialization would copy hundreds of MiB to GiB per call. Any GEMM-layout gain is unlikely to repay the copy and allocator pressure.

3. E16 combined FC1 without retained weight.
Rejected for v149. v128 E16 retained combined FC1 segfaulted, and non-retained combination would still materialize about 1 GiB of weight traffic per call. This repeats the combined-weight family in a worse lifetime form.

DUPLICATE_AUDIT

Not duplicate of v136: v136 introduced the padded-row guard but computed row count from `group_offsets`. v149 keeps the guard and changes the metadata source to `group_sizes`.

Not duplicate of v141: v141 only added `del up_output` and `del packed_output`.

Not duplicate of v115/v145 prescale: no routed-weight movement.

Not duplicate of v130 preallocation: no `torch.bmm`, no `out=`, no new workspace.

Not duplicate of v134: no weight transpose hoist.

Not duplicate of v142/v144/v147: no host per-expert loops, no row-split BMM, no static M reduction.

SELECTED_ARCHITECTURE

Implement `v149_swiglu_group_sizes_metadata` from `v141_release_fc1_transients.py`.

The architecture remains packed dense MoE with fixed `[E,192,H]`, standard high-level `@` BMMs, guarded TileLang SwiGLU, chunked FC2, and weighted unpack. The only dataflow change is that SwiGLU receives `group_sizes` directly, matching pack/unpack metadata, instead of recomputing the same value from prefix offsets.

NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS

1. In `_swiglu_kernel`, replace the parameter:
   `group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32)`
   with:
   `group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32)`

2. In the SwiGLU kernel body, replace:
   `group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]`
   with:
   `group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]`

3. In `run_kernel`, replace:
   `swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)`
   with:
   `swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, gate_output)`

4. Do not change `_EXPERT_BLOCK_M`, FC1 BMMs, FC2 chunk sizes, pack/unpack kernels, `del` placement, or workspace policy.

DATAFLOW_PROOF

For every expert `e`, the contract already provides `group_sizes[e] == group_offsets[e+1] - group_offsets[e]`. Pack and unpack already trust `group_sizes[e]` for valid-row bounds. Therefore using the same value in SwiGLU writes exactly the same valid activation rows and skips exactly the same padded rows as v141.

All BMM inputs and outputs remain shape-identical:
`packed_input @ gate_w.T`, `packed_input @ up_w.T`, and chunked `activation @ down_w.T` are unchanged.

MEMORY_LIFETIME

No new tensors. No retained weights. No new workspace. Same `up_output` deletion and per-chunk `packed_output` deletion as v141. Kernel argument changes only metadata pointer passed to SwiGLU.

EXPECTED_GAIN

Small but credible: likely 0.0-0.3%, best chance on E64/E32 where SwiGLU has more expert/block programs. The gain source is reduced metadata traffic/integer work in the guarded SwiGLU kernel. This will probably be score-neutral, but it is the strongest safe non-duplicate experiment left under the constraints.

CORRECTNESS_RISKS

Low. Main risk is if OJ metadata violates `group_sizes == diff(group_offsets)`, but pack/unpack already depend on `group_sizes`, so such a violation would already make v141 internally inconsistent. C500 should compare all three official shapes.

PERFORMANCE_RISKS

Low. Passing a different tensor pointer may compile to identical or near-identical code. If compiler caching or lowering is sensitive, timing may be neutral or slightly noisy. It should not regress materially because launch count, tensor allocation, and BMM calls are unchanged.

IMPLEMENTATION_INSTRUCTIONS

Create `v149_swiglu_group_sizes_metadata.py` as a copy of `v141_release_fc1_transients.py`.

Apply only the three source edits listed in `NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS`.

Do not inspect or use `xpuoj/` or old `fused_moe_gemm/`.

C500_GATE

Run all three C500 correctness cases. Require pass, unchanged input tensors, zero padded final output behavior, and max errors within the v141 envelope.

XPUOJ_GATE

Submit only after C500 passes. Require current XPUOJ Accepted. Compare timings against v141 `2.024 / 3.695 / 6.563` and the known v136/v141 noise envelope.

   `group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32)`
   with:
   `group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32)`

2. In the SwiGLU kernel body, replace:
   `group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]`
   with:
   `group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]`

3. In `run_kernel`, replace:
   `swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)`
   with:
   `swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, gate_output)`

4. Do not change `_EXPERT_BLOCK_M`, FC1 BMMs, FC2 chunk sizes, pack/unpack kernels, `del` placement, or workspace policy.

DATAFLOW_PROOF

For every expert `e`, the contract already provides `group_sizes[e] == group_offsets[e+1] - group_offsets[e]`. Pack and unpack already trust `group_sizes[e]` for valid-row bounds. Therefore using the same value in SwiGLU writes exactly the same valid activation rows and skips exactly the same padded rows as v141.

All BMM inputs and outputs remain shape-identical:
`packed_input @ gate_w.T`, `packed_input @ up_w.T`, and chunked `activation @ down_w.T` are unchanged.

MEMORY_LIFETIME

No new tensors. No retained weights. No new workspace. Same `up_output` deletion and per-chunk `packed_output` deletion as v141. Kernel argument changes only metadata pointer passed to SwiGLU.

EXPECTED_GAIN

Small but credible: likely 0.0-0.3%, best chance on E64/E32 where SwiGLU has more expert/block programs. The gain source is reduced metadata traffic/integer work in the guarded SwiGLU kernel. This will probably be score-neutral, but it is the strongest safe non-duplicate experiment left under the constraints.

CORRECTNESS_RISKS

Low. Main risk is if OJ metadata violates `group_sizes == diff(group_offsets)`, but pack/unpack already depend on `group_sizes`, so such a violation would already make v141 internally inconsistent. C500 should compare all three official shapes.

PERFORMANCE_RISKS

Low. Passing a different tensor pointer may compile to identical or near-identical code. If compiler caching or lowering is sensitive, timing may be neutral or slightly noisy. It should not regress materially because launch count, tensor allocation, and BMM calls are unchanged.

IMPLEMENTATION_INSTRUCTIONS

Create `v149_swiglu_group_sizes_metadata.py` as a copy of `v141_release_fc1_transients.py`.

Apply only the three source edits listed in `NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS`.

Do not inspect or use `xpuoj/` or old `fused_moe_gemm/`.

C500_GATE

Run all three C500 correctness cases. Require pass, unchanged input tensors, zero padded final output behavior, and max errors within the v141 envelope.

XPUOJ_GATE

Submit only after C500 passes. Require current XPUOJ Accepted. Compare timings against v141 `2.024 / 3.695 / 6.563` and the known v136/v141 noise envelope.

FINAL_GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT
